# Conformance status

Generated by `./run` — do not edit by hand. Reference git: v2.56.1.

- **reference**: the script run against the reference git. A case that does not
  pass there is not a valid check and is excluded from the rgit columns.
- **fallback**: calls that match a line of `implemented.txt` run rgit, all other
  calls are forwarded to the reference git (both listed per case below).
  **via rgit** counts the passing valid cases in which at least one call ran rgit.
- **strict**: everything runs through rgit.
